3784 — cross arms push up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3784 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

A push-up with your hands crossed under your chest. It puts extra load on the triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start in a high plank with your hands crossed under your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3784.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Start in a high plank with your hands crossed under your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3784.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use toe support under the documented support leg or legs and keep the pelvis level; when a lifted-leg source leaves the first side open, stage the right leg first and then mirror.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 3784.json#/instructions/0

For this floor-support pose, stage the documented hand, forearm, foot or knee supports on a level ground plane unless the source names another surface; this is an authored support choice. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3784.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start in a high plank with your hands crossed under your chest. 3784.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your body in a straight line from head to heels. 3784.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower your chest toward the floor with your elbows close. 3784.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press back up until your arms are straight. 3784.json#/instructions/3
  5. Switch which hand is on top each set. 3784.json#/instructions/4

3785 — incline push-up (on box)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3785 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

An incline push-up with the hands on a box. It trains the chest, shoulders and triceps with less load than a floor push-up.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a high-plank blocking pose on the documented support, with a long aligned torso; preserve any source-defined low-plank or lifted-leg modification.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 3785.json#/name

Arms Hands

Place your hands on the edge of a box or elevated surface, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3785.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use toe support under the documented support leg or legs and keep the pelvis level; when a lifted-leg source leaves the first side open, stage the right leg first and then mirror.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 3785.json#/name

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Plyometric box, Raised platform/deficit block. For this floor-support pose, stage the documented hand, forearm, foot or knee supports on a level ground plane unless the source names another surface; this is an authored support choice. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3785.json#/name

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Place your hands on the edge of a box or elevated surface, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 3785.json#/instructions/0
  2. Extend your legs behind you, resting on the balls of your feet, creating a straight line from your head to your heels. 3785.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower your chest towards the box by bending your elbows, keeping your body in a straight line. 3785.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the bottom, then push yourself back up to the starting position by straightening your arms. 3785.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3785.json#/instructions/4

3786 — resistance band squat with single arm row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3786 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A squat combined with a one-arm band row. It trains your legs and your back together.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Anchor the band at a low point and hold the handle in one hand. Stand tall with feet shoulder width apart and the band under light tension. Sit your hips back and down into a squat while keeping your chest up. Stand up and, at the top, pull the handle to your ribs with your elbow close to your body, then let it return slowly.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3786.json#/instructions/0, 3786.json#/instructions/1, 3786.json#/instructions/2, 3786.json#/instructions/3

3787 — resistance band deadlift with single arm row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3787 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A hip hinge paired with a one-arm band row. It works your posterior chain and your upper back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Anchor the band at a low point and hold the handle in one hand. Stand with soft knees and the band under light tension.

  2. Stand with soft knees and the band under light tension. 3787.json#/instructions/1

3788 — resistance band high knee lunge with single arm row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3788 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A lunge that finishes with a high knee, combined with a one-arm band row. It builds leg strength and balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 3788.json#/name

Arms Hands

Anchor the band at a low point and hold the handle in one hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3788.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 3788.json#/name

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3788.json#/name

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Anchor the band at a low point and hold the handle in one hand. 3788.json#/instructions/0
  2. Step back into a lunge and lower until your front thigh is about parallel. 3788.json#/instructions/1
  3. Push through your front foot and drive the rear knee up to hip height. 3788.json#/instructions/2
  4. Row the handle to your ribs while balancing on one leg. 3788.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return to the start under control. Finish your reps, then switch sides. 3788.json#/instructions/4

3789 — resistance band single stiff leg deadlift with single arm row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3789 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A single-leg hip hinge with a one-arm band row. It challenges your balance and trains hamstrings, glutes and back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Anchor the band at a low point and hold the handle in one hand. Stand on one leg with a soft knee and the band under light tension.

  7. Lower the dumbbell back down to the starting position in a controlled manner. 3888.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch to the other arm. 3888.json#/instructions/7
